### Here is how to test organizational ideas about serving Latine audiences

So, your public media organization aspires to expand its audience into Hispanic communities. You have an employee or two interested in leading the campaign. Maybe you can put some money or staff hours aside to make it happen. **How do you do it without flopping? 💔**

This OIGO, we’ll put your ideas for serving Latina/o/e/x audiences with public media through the paces. 👟 Whether you believe translations are the way or a bilingual podcast is the right approach, it is crucial to test your hypotheses. Asking questions early will save you a lot of time and money.

**Pressing your theories may have one other benefit, too.** 😲

The costs of bad Latina/o/e/x outreach can be fatal for your organization’s strategy. We hone in a lot on time and resources, but also we’re talking relationships. There is nothing harder to bounce back from than a flawed vision that may seem insensitive or hurtful to established voices. 😤 Spanish-language media creators and leaders feeling like you stole their ideas, copied their coverage with your branding, or behaved like they weren’t there before you will damage your credibility, especially if they have the trust that you don’t have yet. ☝🏼 In hearing from aggrieved parties, I can say resentments can linger. Memories are long, and repairing mistakes can be an ongoing struggle.

I have been fortunate to have many conversations with public radio and television groups that have Latina/o/e/x engagement as a priority. Where the good intention gets messed up is in doing the necessary interrogation of your big ideas.

**Ready?** Let’s start making a list. 🖊️

![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!cChV!,w_1456,c_limit,f_webp,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F3a709097-685e-4227-99da-a02d075de76e_500x280.gif)

### ☑️ Is your community actually underserved?

You’d be surprised how many organizations think their Hispanic communities are underserved by local journalism, but can’t name a single Spanish-language outlet in their area or a nonprofit they can call to verify this notion. 🤯

At best, the suggestion that Hispanics have no news sources is not okay because you need to do your homework. At worst, this attitude can doom your projects. 😔 **Communities of color have seen before:** someone from outside assuming they need something they never asked for, or trying to coopt things the community already uses solely for the purpose of meeting their own growth/revenue objectives.

Before you start anything in this vein, better to do some looking around. 👀

- What Spanish-language legacy media exists in your area?
	- If it’s TV or radio, what are their audience sizes (generally findable via Googling audience ratings)?
		- If it’s a newspaper, what is its distribution and/or subscription size?
- Are there newsletters, WhatsApp groups or email lists used by the community?
- What do local Latina/o/e/x businesses say they use to get the word out?
- Are there social media influencers/channels devoted to the local Hispanic community? How big are they?

Once you have an idea of what community media exists, you can review your initial premise.

### ☑️ Are you making mission assumptions?

A reflexive response I’ve heard often is that public media’s work is needed because it’s mission-driven journalism. I ask: *as opposed to what?* 🤔

Published in 2019, the Center for Community Media’s [State of the Latino News Media](https://thelatinomediareport.journalism.cuny.edu/) report gives you a glimpse of the diversity of the space serving Hispanics. 🩺 This does not get into the deep commercial industry. You may discover existing media outlets center the Latina/o/e/x community in coverage and have missions as well.

Yes, there *are* trash Latina/o/e/x media outlets. 🗑️ As noted in [KQED’s research](https://oigo.substack.com/p/latino-audience-research-kqed), the audience often feels dissatisfied with Spanish-language media. Yet, your endeavors will benefit when you understand the local Spanish-language market and can frame your aspirations in clear, non-savior-y language.

![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!dvGh!,w_1456,c_limit,f_webp,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F0b752e63-9431-4955-8fc4-a2e537a7de3c_1600x900.png)

### ☑️ What does your community want/need?

Anyone leading up an initiative should be able to say what they know of community needs, especially as it relates to a Latina/o/e/x engagement effort. 📰 Questions you should ask and answer:

- Based on our conversations, what do we hear people want and need most from media?
- Is the demand largest for content (i.e. informational presentation, such as where to get a vaccine) or journalism (i.e. reporting on how vaccines are distributed)?
	- Figure this out: the audience tells us they consume (news, entertainment, sports, culture) in this order.
- What community needs/questions can we most effectively address with the resources we have?

**Okay, you may not get all those answers immediately.** 📳 Your objective is to understand your community better, so it can help guide your new initiative to the greatest success.

### ☑️ How are you thinking of translations?

**Translations are the go-to solution for many public media organizations.** 🔉 There are tons of reasonable arguments for such. You’ve got the stories done already, so making them available in another language extends their reach. You also might not have capacity to create enough original Spanish-language content to foster a habit among audiences, but repackaging English-language reporting represents a way to help with this dilemma.

**On the other hand, there are inquisitions to go with translations.** They’re no substitute for exclusive content. Translations for your website may need to be part of a bigger push to reach [digitally savvy Hispanics](https://www.pewresearch.org/fact-tank/2021/07/16/home-broadband-adoption-computer-ownership-vary-by-race-ethnicity-in-the-u-s/). Some questions to ask about your theory:

- Do you know whether translations will be read, or are what the community wants?
	- If yes, who told you this and can you explore it?
- What sorts of content are seen by respondents as best for translation (examples: content vs. journalism, news vs. culture, et al.)?
- What length is likely to get read?
- Will it live on your site or elsewhere?
- Does the audience know your website?
	- If not, what resources will you invest to market it to them?
- Will translations be copy that lives on a website, or on social media, or video/animation?
- How long do you want to do this? Are you budgeting to sustain it?

Translations in and of themselves are a fine solution, but organizations should talk out the bigger picture. Natalie Van Hoozer offers some thoughts in [the last OIGO](https://oigo.substack.com/p/spanish-bilingual-journalism-publicmedia).

![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!QACc!,w_1456,c_limit,f_webp,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F3d72b458-8f87-496b-be7a-f619237315e5_480x202.gif)

### ☑️ Is your future collaborative?

Before venturing out to start something completely new, have you considered joining forces with Spanish-language media in your region? Many public media organizations have opted to partner with Spanish-language media as a way of extending the reach of their journalism and highlighting others locally. **Guess who?** 🙋

- [Aspen Public Radio and El Sol del Valle](https://www.aspenpublicradio.org/2022-09-15/local-celebrations-kick-off-for-hispanic-heritage-month-and-other-news-from-el-sol-del-valle)
- [WFAE and La Noticia](https://www.wfae.org/inside-wfae/2021-07-21/better-news-podcast-shows-how-wfae-la-noticia-partner-to-serve-charlottes-latino-community)
- [WBUR and El Planeta](https://www.wbur.org/inside/2020/09/10/wbur-el-planeta-partner-on-a-pop-up-newsletter-to-get-mass-voters-ready-for-the-elections)
- [WPLN and Nashville Noticias](https://wpln.org/post/wpln-news-announces-spanish-language-partnership-with-nashville-noticias/)
- [WFPL and Al Dia en America](https://www.aldiaenamerica.com/category/wfpl-news/)

…among others.

As you’ll see from these examples, varied arrangements take shape. 🤝 Sometimes, there are content-sharing agreements. At times, there can be pacts to do reporting. Other times, it may be only translation and posting to respective audiences. Each organization should come to the table with an idea of their needs. And, if you're in public media and initiating the call, be generous with what you'll give. The positive word of mouth will pay off.

### 💥 Closing thought

A lot of presumptions about diverse audiences come from a benign place. People are passionate about public media's mission and have faith in our journalism. The trap is that place of not knowing a community. Don't make a guess about what Latina/o/e/x audiences in your town want or need. Talk to a lot of people. Ask many questions. Listen actively and empathetically. And, above all, press what your ideas are to the farthest point you can. Your public media organization can only win through such trials. 🟢

![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!OxTT!,w_1456,c_limit,f_webp,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F551fe6d0-bb7e-4718-aaa2-28051d86b54e_1920x1280.jpeg)

## Cafecito: stories to discuss ☕

- Five Hispanic publishers, including [one featured in OIGO](https://oigo.substack.com/p/activism-journalism-objectivity), talk about their [processes and learnings](https://localmedia.org/2022/10/national-hispanic-american-heritage-month-five-publishers-serving-latinx-hispanic-and-spanish-speaking-communities/) while reporting on their communities.
- Online outlet [Radio Jornalera](https://www.mprnews.org/story/2022/10/03/radio-program-reaches-out-to-latinos-living-between-worlds) gets coverage from Minnesota Public Radio. Of interest: the focus is not on broadcast licenses, but a podcast network. 🎙️
- “Is it harmful to society for Facebook to have an effective monopoly on the internet and communications in the Global South?” 💻 According to [a recent report](https://cfde140b-3710-4a65-aa9a-48b5868a02dd.usrfiles.com/ugd/3ba8a1_291accfaef524f0591d3c3905df22c53.pdf) by the Center for International Policy, the social media company is increasingly influential in Latin America.
- Does greater public interest in the threats facing Mexican journalists lie in more stories? 🗞️ One journalist says Mexican citizens [don’t care as much](https://latamjournalismreview.org/articles/the-press-must-reconnect-with-the-audience-so-they-demand-safety-for-journalists-former-ap-chief-in-mexico-katherine-corcoran-said/) about the spate of crimes against reporters because journalism is not as relevant in their lives. Better, more thorough storytelling could go a long way.
- Speaking of Mexico, [here’s a new audio/video project](https://pudding.cool/2022/09/cdmx/) sharing the sounds of the streets of Mexico City. 🔔

---

![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!ULT7!,w_1456,c_limit,f_webp,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F60dbdf84-21be-475b-b08e-a4a636a833d9_480x480.gif)

## El radar: try this 📡

- **Seek out local thoughts on guns.** Florida Democrats suggest [targeting gun safety](https://apnews.com/article/gun-violence-miami-florida-donald-trump-3c4ba429fa657c018bc468f880e984f5) is a way to win back Latinos amid GOP gains in the state. 🔫 Groups like [Voto Latino](https://votolatino.org/issues/gun-violence-prevention/) have long advocated this strategy. How do Hispanic residents feel about this topic?
- **Ask who’s returning to accents.** For the Texas Standard, Kristen Cabrera chronicles the resurgence of Hispanics [adding accents back](https://www.texasstandard.org/stories/accent-marks-making-comeback-latino-last-names/) to their names. Assimilation and technology glitches seemed to erase their use in surnames and elsewhere. ✍🏼 This is shifting.
- **See about organ donation and Latina/o/e/x residents.** Gustavo Sagrero for KUNR talks with experts about [organ donation](https://www.kunr.org/public-health/2022-09-28/multiethnic-organ-donors-rising-northern-nevada-california-kidneys-high-demand-latino-community) and diverse communities. 😷 Donors with similar ethnic backgrounds as organ recipients are more likely to be tissue matches. For the Latino community, the demand for kidneys is high.
- **Talk with Latino candidates.** Gabriela Lozada at New Hampshire Public Radio [covered a panel](https://www.nhpr.org/nh-news/2022-09-07/latino-candidates-running-for-state-local-offices-explain-their-proposals-for-their-community) of Hispanic candidates in the region. 🗳️ If these sorts of roundtables are happening near you, are you covering them? Can you convene one?
- **… And with voters. 🗣️** The team at [Colorado Public Radio](https://www.cpr.org/podcast-episode/colorados-latino-voters-and-the-2022-races/) asked local Latinos how they felt about elections and candidates’ efforts to engage them.

**See you soon with the first birthday newsletter! 🎂**

class="pencraft pc-position-fixed pc-reset showFocus-sk_vEm pencraft trigger-V8d1vI fixed-n4RrZu"><div class="pencraft pc-display-flex pc-flexDirection-column pc-gap-12 pc-paddingLeft-8 pc-paddingRight-8 pc-paddingTop-12 pc-paddingBottom-12 pc-alignItems-flex-end pc-reset"><div class="line-DsYVXw indent-2-yiZ6hb"></div><div class="line-DsYVXw indent-2-yiZ6hb"></div><div class="line-DsYVXw indent-2-yiZ6hb"></div><div class="line-DsYVXw indent-2-yiZ6hb"></div><div class="line-DsYVXw indent-2-yiZ6hb"></div><div class="line-DsYVXw indent-2-yiZ6hb"></div><div class="line-DsYVXw indent-1-vdeuvl"></div><div class="line-DsYVXw"></div><div class="line-DsYVXw"></div></div></button><div><div class="available-content"><div dir="auto" class="body markup"><p><span>So, your public media organization aspires to expand its audience into Hispanic communities. You have an employee or two interested in leading the campaign. Maybe you can put some money or staff hours aside to make it happen. </span><strong>How do you do it without flopping? 💔</strong></p><p>This OIGO, we’ll put your ideas for serving Latina/o/e/x audiences with public media through the paces. 👟 Whether you believe translations are the way or a bilingual podcast is the right approach, it is crucial to test your hypotheses. Asking questions early will save you a lot of time and money.</p><p><strong>Pressing your theories may have one other benefit, too.</strong><span> 😲</span></p><p>The costs of bad Latina/o/e/x outreach can be fatal for your organization’s strategy. We hone in a lot on time and resources, but also we’re talking relationships. There is nothing harder to bounce back from than a flawed vision that may seem insensitive or hurtful to established voices. 😤 Spanish-language media creators and leaders feeling like you stole their ideas, copied their coverage with your branding, or behaved like they weren’t there before you will damage your credibility, especially if they have the trust that you don’t have yet. ☝🏼 In hearing from aggrieved parties, I can say resentments can linger. Memories are long, and repairing mistakes can be an ongoing struggle.</p><p>I have been fortunate to have many conversations with public radio and television groups that have Latina/o/e/x engagement as a priority. Where the good intention gets messed up is in doing the necessary interrogation of your big ideas.</p><p><strong>Ready?</strong><span> Let’s start making a list. 🖊️</span></p><div y2="14"></line></svg></button></div></div></div></a></figure></div><h3 class="header-anchor-post">☑️ Is your community actually underserved?<div class="pencraft pc-display-flex pc-alignItems-center pc-position-absolute pc-reset header-anchor-parent"><div class="pencraft pc-display-contents pc-reset pubTheme-yiXxQA"><div id="§is-your-community-actually-underserved" class="pencraft pc-reset header-anchor offset-top"></div><button tabindex="0" type="button" aria-label="Link" data-href="https://oigo.us/i/52995094/is-your-community-actually-underserved" class="pencraft pc-reset pencraft iconButton-mq_Et5 iconButtonBase-dJGHgN buttonBase-GK1x3M buttonStyle-r7yGCK size_sm-G3LciD priority_secondary-S63h9o"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></button></div></div></h3><p>You’d be surprised how many organizations think their Hispanic communities are underserved by local journalism, but can’t name a single Spanish-language outlet in their area or a nonprofit they can call to verify this notion. 🤯</p><p><span>At best, the suggestion that Hispanics have no news sources is not okay because you need to do your homework. At worst, this attitude can doom your projects. 😔 </span><strong>Communities of color have seen before:</strong><span> someone from outside assuming they need something they never asked for, or trying to coopt things the community already uses solely for the purpose of meeting their own growth/revenue objectives.</span></p><p>Before you start anything in this vein, better to do some looking around. 👀</p><ul><li><p>What Spanish-language legacy media exists in your area?</p><ul><li><p>If it’s TV or radio, what are their audience sizes (generally findable via Googling audience ratings)?</p></li><li><p>If it’s a newspaper, what is its distribution and/or subscription size?</p></li></ul></li><li><p>Are there newsletters, WhatsApp groups or email lists used by the community?</p></li><li><p>What do local Latina/o/e/x businesses say they use to get the word out?</p></li><li><p>Are there social media influencers/channels devoted to the local Hispanic community? How big are they?</p></li></ul><p>Once you have an idea of what community media exists, you can review your initial premise.</p><h3 class="header-anchor-post">☑️ Are you making mission assumptions?<div class="pencraft pc-display-flex pc-alignItems-center pc-position-absolute pc-reset header-anchor-parent"><div class="pencraft pc-display-contents pc-reset pubTheme-yiXxQA"><div id="§are-you-making-mission-assumptions" class="pencraft pc-reset header-anchor offset-top"></div><button tabindex="0" type="button" aria-label="Link" data-href="https://oigo.us/i/52995094/are-you-making-mission-assumptions" class="pencraft pc-reset pencraft iconButton-mq_Et5 iconButtonBase-dJGHgN buttonBase-GK1x3M buttonStyle-r7yGCK size_sm-G3LciD priority_secondary-S63h9o"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></button></div></div></h3><p><span>A reflexive response I’ve heard often is that public media’s work is needed because it’s mission-driven journalism. I ask: </span><em>as opposed to what? </em><span>🤔</span></p><p><span>Published in 2019, the Center for Community Media’s </span><a href="https://thelatinomediareport.journalism.cuny.edu/">State of the Latino News Media</a><span> report gives you a glimpse of the diversity of the space serving Hispanics. 🩺 This does not get into the deep commercial industry. You may discover existing media outlets center the Latina/o/e/x community in coverage and have missions as well.</span></p><p><span>Yes, there </span><em>are</em><span> trash Latina/o/e/x media outlets. 🗑️ As noted in </span><a href="https://oigo.substack.com/p/latino-audience-research-kqed">KQED’s research</a><span>, the audience often feels dissatisfied with Spanish-language media. Yet, your endeavors will benefit when you understand the local Spanish-language market and can frame your aspirations in clear, non-savior-y language.</span></p><div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><g><path d="M2.53001 7.81595C3.49179 4.73911 6.43281 2.5 9.91173 2.5C13.1684 2.5 15.9537 4.46214 17.0852 7.23684L17.6179 8.67647M17.6179 8.67647L18.5002 3 21 3 21 9"></polyline><polyline points="9 21 3 21 3 15"></polyline><line x1="21" x2="14" y1="3" y2="10"></line><line x1="3" x2="10" y1="21" y2="14"></line></svg></button></div></div></div></a></figure></div><h3 class="header-anchor-post">☑️ What does your community want/need?<div class="pencraft pc-display-flex pc-alignItems-center pc-position-absolute pc-reset header-anchor-parent"><div class="pencraft pc-display-contents pc-reset pubTheme-yiXxQA"><div id="§what-does-your-community-wantneed" class="pencraft pc-reset header-anchor offset-top"></div><button tabindex="0" type="button" aria-label="Link" data-href="https://oigo.us/i/52995094/what-does-your-community-wantneed" class="pencraft pc-reset pencraft iconButton-mq_Et5 iconButtonBase-dJGHgN buttonBase-GK1x3M buttonStyle-r7yGCK size_sm-G3LciD priority_secondary-S63h9o"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></button></div></div></h3><p>Anyone leading up an initiative should be able to say what they know of community needs, especially as it relates to a Latina/o/e/x engagement effort. 📰 Questions you should ask and answer:</p><ul><li><p>Based on our conversations, what do we hear people want and need most from media?</p></li><li><p>Is the demand largest for content (i.e. informational presentation, such as where to get a vaccine) or journalism (i.e. reporting on how vaccines are distributed)?</p><ul><li><p>Figure this out: the audience tells us they consume (news, entertainment, sports, culture) in this order.</p></li></ul></li><li><p>What community needs/questions can we most effectively address with the resources we have?</p></li></ul><p><strong>Okay, you may not get all those answers immediately.</strong><span> 📳 Your objective is to understand your community better, so it can help guide your new initiative to the greatest success.</span></p><h3 class="header-anchor-post">☑️ How are you thinking of translations?<div class="pencraft pc-display-flex pc-alignItems-center pc-position-absolute pc-reset header-anchor-parent"><div class="pencraft pc-display-contents pc-reset pubTheme-yiXxQA"><div id="§how-are-you-thinking-of-translations" class="pencraft pc-reset header-anchor offset-top"></div><button tabindex="0" type="button" aria-label="Link" data-href="https://oigo.us/i/52995094/how-are-you-thinking-of-translations" class="pencraft pc-reset pencraft iconButton-mq_Et5 iconButtonBase-dJGHgN buttonBase-GK1x3M buttonStyle-r7yGCK size_sm-G3LciD priority_secondary-S63h9o"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></button></div></div></h3><p><strong>Translations are the go-to solution for many public media organizations.</strong><span> 🔉 There are tons of reasonable arguments for such. You’ve got the stories done already, so making them available in another language extends their reach. You also might not have capacity to create enough original Spanish-language content to foster a habit among audiences, but repackaging English-language reporting represents a way to help with this dilemma.</span></p><p><strong>On the other hand, there are inquisitions to go with translations.</strong><span> They’re no substitute for exclusive content. Translations for your website may need to be part of a bigger push to reach </span><a href="https://www.pewresearch.org/fact-tank/2021/07/16/home-broadband-adoption-computer-ownership-vary-by-race-ethnicity-in-the-u-s/">digitally savvy Hispanics</a><span>. Some questions to ask about your theory:</span></p><ul><li><p>Do you know whether translations will be read, or are what the community wants?</p><ul><li><p>If yes, who told you this and can you explore it? </p></li></ul></li><li><p>What sorts of content are seen by respondents as best for translation (examples: content vs. journalism, news vs. culture, et al.)?</p></li><li><p>What length is likely to get read?</p></li><li><p>Will it live on your site or elsewhere?</p></li><li><p>Does the audience know your website?</p><ul><li><p>If not, what resources will you invest to market it to them?</p></li></ul></li><li><p>Will translations be copy that lives on a website, or on social media, or video/animation?</p></li><li><p>How long do you want to do this? Are you budgeting to sustain it?</p></li></ul><p><span>Translations in and of themselves are a fine solution, but organizations should talk out the bigger picture. Natalie Van Hoozer offers some thoughts in </span><a href="https://oigo.substack.com/p/spanish-bilingual-journalism-publicmedia">the last OIGO</a><span>.</span></p><div class="captioned-image-container"><figure><a target="_blank" 1456w"></picture><div></div></div></a></figure></div><h3 class="header-anchor-post">☑️ Is your future collaborative?<div class="pencraft pc-display-flex pc-alignItems-center pc-position-absolute pc-reset header-anchor-parent"><div class="pencraft pc-display-contents pc-reset pubTheme-yiXxQA"><div id="§is-your-future-collaborative" class="pencraft pc-reset header-anchor offset-top"></div><button tabindex="0" type="button" aria-label="Link" data-href="https://oigo.us/i/52995094/is-your-future-collaborative" class="pencraft pc-reset pencraft iconButton-mq_Et5 iconButtonBase-dJGHgN buttonBase-GK1x3M buttonStyle-r7yGCK size_sm-G3LciD priority_secondary-S63h9o"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></button></div></div></h3><p><span>Before venturing out to start something completely new, have you considered joining forces with Spanish-language media in your region? Many public media organizations have opted to partner with Spanish-language media as a way of extending the reach of their journalism and highlighting others locally. </span><strong>Guess who?</strong><span> 🙋</span></p><ul><li><p><a href="https://www.aspenpublicradio.org/2022-09-15/local-celebrations-kick-off-for-hispanic-heritage-month-and-other-news-from-el-sol-del-valle">Aspen Public Radio and El Sol del Valle</a></p></li><li><p><a href="https://www.wfae.org/inside-wfae/2021-07-21/better-news-podcast-shows-how-wfae-la-noticia-partner-to-serve-charlottes-latino-community">WFAE and La Noticia</a></p></li><li><p><a href="https://www.wbur.org/inside/2020/09/10/wbur-el-planeta-partner-on-a-pop-up-newsletter-to-get-mass-voters-ready-for-the-elections">WBUR and El Planeta</a></p></li><li><p><a href="https://wpln.org/post/wpln-news-announces-spanish-language-partnership-with-nashville-noticias/">WPLN and Nashville Noticias</a></p></li><li><p><a href="https://www.aldiaenamerica.com/category/wfpl-news/">WFPL and Al Dia en America</a></p></li></ul><p>…among others.</p><p>As you’ll see from these examples, varied arrangements take shape. 🤝 Sometimes, there are content-sharing agreements. At times, there can be pacts to do reporting. Other times, it may be only translation and posting to respective audiences. Each organization should come to the table with an idea of their needs. And, if you're in public media and initiating the call, be generous with what you'll give. The positive word of mouth will pay off.</p><h3 class="header-anchor-post">💥 Closing thought<div class="pencraft pc-display-flex pc-alignItems-center pc-position-absolute pc-reset header-anchor-parent"><div class="pencraft pc-display-contents pc-reset pubTheme-yiXxQA"><div id="§closing-thought" class="pencraft pc-reset header-anchor offset-top"></div><button tabindex="0" type="button" aria-label="Link" data-href="https://oigo.us/i/52995094/closing-thought" class="pencraft pc-reset pencraft iconButton-mq_Et5 iconButtonBase-dJGHgN buttonBase-GK1x3M buttonStyle-r7yGCK size_sm-G3LciD priority_secondary-S63h9o"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></button></div></div></h3><p>A lot of presumptions about diverse audiences come from a benign place. People are passionate about public media's mission and have faith in our journalism. The trap is that place of not knowing a community. Don't make a guess about what Latina/o/e/x audiences in your town want or need. Talk to a lot of people. Ask many questions. Listen actively and empathetically. And, above all, press what your ideas are to the farthest point you can. Your public media organization can only win through such trials. 🟢</p><div class="captioned-image-container"><figure><a target="_blank" href="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!OxTT!,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbucketeer-e05bbc84-baa3-437e-9518-adb32be77984.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F551fe6d0-bb7e-4718-aaa2-28051d86b54e_1920x1280.jpeg" header-anchor-parent"><div class="pencraft pc-display-contents pc-reset pubTheme-yiXxQA"><div id="§la-proxima" class="pencraft pc-reset header-anchor offset-top"></div><button tabindex="0" Felix Contreras of NPR’s </span><a href="https://www.npr.org/sections/altlatino/">AltLatino</a><span> join us to talk about the relaunch of the program, what sparked it, and more. Remaking a legacy brand sounds like something you can learn from, </span><em>no</em><span>? Let’s do it.</span></p><p><strong>The next newsletter marks OIGO’s first birthday! 🎉🎉🎉</strong><span> Lots of thoughts then. And if you have words to share, I’ll collect them for that edition. pc-position-absolute pc-reset header-anchor-parent"><div class="pencraft pc-display-contents pc-reset pubTheme-yiXxQA"><div id="§cafecito-stories-to-discuss" class="pencraft pc-reset header-anchor offset-top"></div><button tabindex="0" type="button" aria-label="Link" data-href="https://oigo.us/i/52995094/cafecito-stories-to-discuss" class="pencraft pc-reset pencraft iconButton-mq_Et5 iconButtonBase-dJGHgN buttonBase-GK1x3M buttonStyle-r7yGCK size_sm-G3LciD priority_secondary-S63h9o"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></button></div></div></h1><ul><li><p><span>Five Hispanic publishers, including </span><a href="https://oigo.substack.com/p/activism-journalism-objectivity">one featured in OIGO</a><span>, talk about their </span><a href="https://localmedia.org/2022/10/national-hispanic-american-heritage-month-five-publishers-serving-latinx-hispanic-and-spanish-speaking-communities/">processes and learnings</a><span> while reporting on their communities.</span></p></li><li><p><span>Online outlet </span><a href="https://www.mprnews.org/story/2022/10/03/radio-program-reaches-out-to-latinos-living-between-worlds">Radio Jornalera</a><span> gets coverage from Minnesota Public Radio. Of interest: the focus is not on broadcast licenses, but a podcast network. 🎙️</span></p></li><li><p><span>“Is it harmful to society for Facebook to have an effective monopoly on the internet and communications in the Global South?” 💻 According to </span><a href="https://cfde140b-3710-4a65-aa9a-48b5868a02dd.usrfiles.com/ugd/3ba8a1_291accfaef524f0591d3c3905df22c53.pdf">a recent report</a><span> by the Center for International Policy, the social media company is increasingly influential in Latin America.</span></p></li><li><p><span>Does greater public interest in the threats facing Mexican journalists lie in more stories? 🗞️ One journalist says Mexican citizens </span><a href="https://latamjournalismreview.org/articles/the-press-must-reconnect-with-the-audience-so-they-demand-safety-for-journalists-former-ap-chief-in-mexico-katherine-corcoran-said/">don’t care as much</a><span> about the spate of crimes against reporters because journalism is not as relevant in their lives. Better, more thorough storytelling could go a long way.</span></p></li><li><p><span>Speaking of Mexico, </span><a href="https://pudding.cool/2022/09/cdmx/">here’s a new audio/video project</a><span> sharing the sounds of the streets of Mexico City. 🔔</span></p></li></ul><div data-component-name="SubscribeWidget" class="subscribe-widget"><div class="pencraft pc-display-flex pc-reset header-anchor-parent"><div class="pencraft pc-display-contents pc-reset pubTheme-yiXxQA"><div id="§el-radar-try-this" class="pencraft pc-reset header-anchor offset-top"></div><button tabindex="0" type="button" aria-label="Link" data-href="https://oigo.us/i/52995094/el-radar-try-this" class="pencraft pc-reset pencraft iconButton-mq_Et5 iconButtonBase-dJGHgN buttonBase-GK1x3M buttonStyle-r7yGCK size_sm-G3LciD priority_secondary-S63h9o"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></button></div></div></h1><ul><li><p><strong>Seek out local thoughts on guns.</strong><span> Florida Democrats suggest </span><a href="https://apnews.com/article/gun-violence-miami-florida-donald-trump-3c4ba429fa657c018bc468f880e984f5">targeting gun safety</a><span> is a way to win back Latinos amid GOP gains in the state. 🔫 Groups like </span><a href="https://votolatino.org/issues/gun-violence-prevention/">Voto Latino</a><span> have long advocated this strategy. How do Hispanic residents feel about this topic?</span></p></li><li><p><strong>Ask who’s returning to accents.</strong><span> For the Texas Standard, Kristen Cabrera chronicles the resurgence of Hispanics </span><a href="https://www.texasstandard.org/stories/accent-marks-making-comeback-latino-last-names/">adding accents back</a><span> to their names. Assimilation and technology glitches seemed to erase their use in surnames and elsewhere. ✍🏼 This is shifting.</span></p></li><li><p><strong>See about organ donation and Latina/o/e/x residents.</strong><span> Gustavo Sagrero for KUNR talks with experts about </span><a href="https://www.kunr.org/public-health/2022-09-28/multiethnic-organ-donors-rising-northern-nevada-california-kidneys-high-demand-latino-community">organ donation</a><span> and diverse communities. 😷 Donors with similar ethnic backgrounds as organ recipients are more likely to be tissue matches. For the Latino community, the demand for kidneys is high.</span></p></li><li><p><strong>Talk with Latino candidates.</strong><span> Gabriela Lozada at New Hampshire Public Radio </span><a href="https://www.nhpr.org/nh-news/2022-09-07/latino-candidates-running-for-state-local-offices-explain-their-proposals-for-their-community">covered a panel</a><span> of Hispanic candidates in the region. 🗳️ If these sorts of roundtables are happening near you, are you covering them? Can you convene one?</span></p></li><li><p><strong>… And with voters. 🗣️</strong><span> The team at </span><a href="https://www.cpr.org/podcast-episode/colorados-latino-voters-and-the-2022-races/">Colorado Public Radio</a><span> asked local Latinos how they felt about elections and candidates’ efforts to engage them.</span></p></li></ul><p><strong>See you soon with the first birthday newsletter! 🎂</strong></p></div></div><div class="visibility-check"></div></div><div data-orientation="horizontal" fill=&quot;#292929&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;style&gt;
    .acrobat-button-container {
        position: fixed;
        right: 0;
        bottom: 36px;
        z-index: 2147483646;
        visibility: visible !important;
        pointer-events: all;
        opacity: 1;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        transition: opacity 0.3s ease-in-out;
    }

    @media print {
        .acrobat-button-container {
            display: none !important;
        }
    }

    .acrobat-button {
        position: relative;
        background: white;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        pointer-events: all;
        touch-action: none;
        box-shadow: 0px 2px 8px 0px #00000029;
        height: 34px;
        width: 34px;
        border: 1px solid transparent;
        background-clip: padding-box, border-box;
        background-origin: border-box;
        background-image: linear-gradient(white, white),
                        linear-gradient(95.85deg, #D73220 0%, #D92361 33%, #7155FA 100%);
        border-radius: 18px;
        justify-content: right;
        margin-right: var(--fab-margin-right, 28px);
        overflow: hidden;
    }

    .acrobat-button-container-transparent {
        opacity: 0.4 !important;
        transition: opacity 0.3s ease-in-out;
    }

    .acrobat-button-container-transparent:hover,
    .acrobat-button-container-transparent:focus,
    .acrobat-button-container-transparent:active {
        opacity: 1;
    }

    .acrobat-button &gt; svg {
        flex-shrink: 0;
        margin: 4px 5px 5px 5px;
        width: 22px;
        height: 22px;
        pointer-events: none;
        fill: #b40000;
    }
    .acrobat-button:has(.close-btn.open),
    .expand-acrobat-button {
        width: 64px;
    }

    .disabled {
        background: #B1B1B1;
    }

    .tooltip-text {
        width: 160px;
        visibility: hidden;
        opacity: 0;
        background-color: #FFFFFF;
        color: #292929;
        border-radius: 7px;
        padding: 4px 1px 5px 9px;
        position: absolute;
        z-index: 1;
        right: 100%;
        top: 50%;
        transform: translateY(-50%);
        margin-right: 10px;
        transition: opacity 0.3s, visibility 0s linear 2s;
        font-size: 12px;
        line-height: 15px;
        font-family: 'adobe-clean', Adobe Clean, sans-serif;
        filter: drop-shadow(0px 1px 6px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12));
    }

    .tooltip-text::after {
       content: '';
       position: absolute;
       top: 50%;
       left: 100%;
       transform: translateY(-50%);
       border-width: 6px;
       border-style: solid;
       border-color: transparent transparent transparent #fff;
    }

    .show-tooltip {
        visibility: visible;
        opacity: 1;
        line-height: 1.5;
        font-family: 'adobe-clean', Adobe Clean, sans-serif;
        transition: opacity 0.3s;
        transition-delay: 500ms;
    }

    .close-btn {
        position: relative;
        display: none;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
    }

    .close-btn svg {
        fill: #464646;
    }

    .close-btn.showCloseButton {
        opacity: 1;
        display: flex;
        justify-content: center;
        width: 24px;
        height: 24px;
        padding: 2px;
    }

    .fab-view-settings-dialog {
        display: none;
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 40px;
        z-index: 1;
    }

    .close-btn:hover {
        background: #E6E6E6;
        border-radius: 50%; /* Keep original circular hover */
        cursor: pointer;
    }

    .fab-view-settings-dialog.showDialog {
        display: block;
    }
    .close-btn .burger-icon {
        display: inline;
        margin-bottom: 2px;
    }
    .close-btn .close-icon {
        display: none;
    }
    .close-btn.open .burger-icon {
        display: none;
    }
    .close-btn.open .close-icon {
        display: inline;
    }
    .tooltip-text .tooltip-text-content {
        font-weight: bold;
    }
    .tooltip-text .tooltip-sub-text-text {
        font-style: italic;
        font-size: 11px;
    }

    /* Home tooltip FTE — icon + layout overrides */
    .fab-home-tooltip {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 12px;
        width: 183px;
        border-radius: 8px;
        padding: 6px 8px 8px 12px;
        font-family: 'adobe-clean', Adobe Clean, sans-serif;
        box-shadow: 0px 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.08), 0px 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.04), 0px 0px 1px rgba(0,0,0,0.08), 0px 3px 5px rgba(116,23,208,0.07);
    }
    .fab-home-tooltip .tooltip-text-content {
        font-family: 'adobe-clean', Adobe Clean, sans-serif;
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: 12px;
        line-height: 16px;
        color: #292929;
        white-space: normal;
        word-wrap: break-word;
        width: 145px;
    }
    .fab-home-tooltip-icon {
        flex-shrink: 0;
        display: block;
        width: 16px;
        height: 16px;
    }

    /* Icon container styles - replaces iframe */
    .icon-container {
        width: 33px;
        height: 34px;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        cursor: grab;
        pointer-events: all;
        flex-shrink: 0;
        background: transparent;
        border: none;
    }

    .icon-container:active {
        cursor: grabbing;
    }

    .fab-svg-icon {
        width: 22px;
        height: 22px;
        padding: 5px 5px 5px 6px;
        pointer-events: none;
        fill: #b40000;
    }

    .draggable-handle {
        display: none;
        position: absolute;
        right: var(--drag-handle-right, 12px);
        cursor: grab;
    }
    .draggable-handle-visible {
        display: flex;
    }

    /* Font face declarations - for proper font loading in Shadow DOM */
    @font-face {
        font-family: 'adobe-clean';
        font-weight: 400;
        src: url('chrome-extension://efaidnbmnnnibpcajpcglclefindmkaj/browser/css/fonts/AdobeClean-Regular.otf') format('opentype');
    }
    @font-face {
        font-family: 'adobe-clean';
        font-weight: 700;
        src: url('chrome-extension://efaidnbmnnnibpcajpcglclefindmkaj/browser/css/fonts/AdobeClean-Bold.otf') format('opentype');
    }
    @font-face {
        font-family: 'AdobeClean-Regular';
        src: url('chrome-extension://efaidnbmnnnibpcajpcglclefindmkaj/browser/css/fonts/AdobeClean-Regular.otf') format('opentype');
    }
    @font-face {
        font-family: 'AdobeClean-Bold';
        src: url('chrome-extension://efaidnbmnnnibpcajpcglclefindmkaj/browser/css/fonts/AdobeClean-Bold.otf') format('opentype');
    }
    body {
        font-family: 'AdobeClean-Regular', adobe-clean, &quot;Adobe Clean&quot;, sans-serif;
    }

    .dialog-container {
        width: 166px;
        background-color: #ffffff;
        border-radius: 4px;
        border: 1px solid #B1B1B1;
        box-shadow: 0px 1px 4px 0px #00000026;
        z-index: 1000;
        padding: 4px;
        position: relative;
        margin: 4px 0px;
    }

    .menu {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 4px;
        list-style-type: none;
        margin: 0;
        padding: 0;
    }

    .menu .menu_item {
        cursor: pointer;
        font-family: 'AdobeClean-Regular', adobe-clean, &quot;Adobe Clean&quot;, sans-serif;
        align-content: center;
        font-size: 14px;
        font-weight: 400;
        line-height: 18.2px;
        text-align: left;
        color: #222222;
        padding: 0px 12px;
        height: 32px;
        width: 140px;
    }

    .menu .menu_item:hover {
        background-color: #eaeaea;
    }

    .preferences {
        font-size: 12px;
        font-weight: 400;
        line-height: 18.2px;
        text-align: left;
        color: #464646;
    }

    .divider {
        border-top: 1px solid #E6E6E6;
    }

    /* React-specific wrappers - minimal */
    .fab-root {
        font-family: 'adobe-clean', Adobe Clean, sans-serif;
    }

    button.close-btn {
        background: transparent;
        border: none;
        cursor: pointer;
        padding: 0;
        z-index: 10; /* Ensure clickable above all other elements */
        pointer-events: auto;
    }

    /* FAB Action Pills - From Figma Design */
    .fab-pills-container {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 42px; /* FAB height (34px) + gap (8px) = 42px */
        right: var(--fab-margin-right, 28px);
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 8px;
        align-items: flex-end;
        width: 142px;
        z-index: 1;
    }

    .fab-pills-container.pills-below {
        bottom: auto;
        top: 42px; /* Same gap when positioned below */
    }

    /* Extend hover area using ::after pseudo-element - doesn't affect visual layout */
    .fab-pills-container::after {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: 100%; /* Start right after the pills content */
        left: 0;
        right: 0;
        height: 8px;
        background: transparent;
        pointer-events: auto;
    }

    .fab-pills-container.pills-below::after {
        top: auto;
        bottom: 100%; /* Start right before the pills when positioned below */
    }

    /* FTE Header - First Time Experience (56px with close button) */
    .fab-fte-header {
        position: relative;
        width: 142px;
        min-height: 56px;
        background: #FFFFFF;
        border-radius: 12px;
        box-shadow: 0px 4px 12px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08), 0px 2px 6px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04), 0px 0px 2px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12);
        overflow: hidden;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
    }

    .fab-fte-background {
        position: absolute;
        left: -13px;
        top: 50%;
        transform: translateY(-50%);
        width: 183.33px;
        height: 126.82px;
        background: url('chrome-extension://efaidnbmnnnibpcajpcglclefindmkaj/browser/images/fab-header-background.svg') no-repeat center;
        background-size: cover;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 0;
    }

    .fab-fte-close {
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        right: 0;
        width: 24px;
        height: 24px;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        background: transparent;
        border: none;
        cursor: pointer;
        z-index: 2;
        color: #222222;
        padding: 0;
    }

    .fab-fte-close:hover {
        background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05);
        border-radius: 12px;
    }

    .fab-fte-close svg {
        width: 8px;
        height: 8px;
    }

    .fab-fte-text {
        position: relative;
        width: 102px;
        font-family: 'AdobeClean-Bold', adobe-clean, sans-serif;
        font-weight: 400; /* Use Bold font file directly, not synthetic bold */
        font-size: 12px;
        line-height: 16px;
        color: #222222; /* Figma spec color */
        z-index: 1;
        white-space: normal;
        word-wrap: break-word;
        margin-left: 16px;
        margin-right: auto;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        -webkit-font-smoothing: subpixel-antialiased; /* Better font rendering */
        -moz-osx-font-smoothing: auto;
    }

    /* Hover Header - Normal state (24px without close button) */
    .fab-hover-header {
        position: relative;
        width: 142px;
        min-height: 24px;
        background: #FFFFFF; /* White background to show the colorful SVG and text */
        border-radius: 12px;
        box-shadow: 0px 4px 12px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08), 0px 2px 6px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04), 0px 0px 2px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12);
        overflow: hidden;
        cursor: pointer;
    }

    .fab-hover-background {
        position: absolute;
        left: -27px;
        top: 50%;
        transform: translateY(-50%);
        width: 212.63px;
        height: 147.09px;
        background: url('chrome-extension://efaidnbmnnnibpcajpcglclefindmkaj/browser/images/fab-header-background.svg') no-repeat center;
        background-size: cover;
        pointer-events: none;
        z-index: 0;
    }

    .fab-hover-text {
        position: relative;
        width: 100%;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        font-family: 'AdobeClean-Bold', adobe-clean, sans-serif;
        font-weight: 400; /* Use Bold font file directly, not synthetic bold */
        font-size: 12px;
        line-height: 15px;
        color: #222222; /* Figma spec color */
        z-index: 1;
        white-space: normal;
        padding: 4px 9px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        -webkit-font-smoothing: subpixel-antialiased; /* Better font rendering */
        -moz-osx-font-smoothing: auto;
    }

    /* Individual Pill Buttons */
    .fab-pill-button {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: row;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 0;
        width: 142px;
        min-height: 24px; /* Min height, allows growth with content */
        background: #FFFFFF;
        border: none;
        border-radius: 12px;
        box-shadow: 0px 4px 12px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08), 0px 2px 6px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04), 0px 0px 2px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12);
        cursor: pointer;
        padding: 0;
        transition: background 0.2s ease;
        overflow: visible;
    }

    .fab-pill-button:hover {
        background: #D5D5D5;
    }

    .fab-pill-button:hover .fab-pill-label,
    .fab-pill-button:hover .fab-pill-icon {
        color: #000000; /* Hover state color from Figma Alias/content/neutral/hover */
    }

    .fab-pill-button:active {
        background: #F4F4F4;
    }

    .fab-pill-icon {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        padding: 4px 7px 4px 10px; /* Exact Figma specs: top right bottom left */
        flex-shrink: 0;
        box-sizing: border-box;
    }

    .fab-pill-icon img {
        width: 16px;
        height: 16px;
        display: block;
        flex-shrink: 0;
        /* SVG icons with currentColor fill will inherit color from parent */
        filter: brightness(0) saturate(100%) invert(13%) sepia(0%) saturate(0%) hue-rotate(196deg) brightness(97%) contrast(97%); /* #222222 */
    }

    .fab-pill-button:hover .fab-pill-icon img {
        filter: brightness(0) saturate(100%); /* #000000 on hover */
    }

    .fab-pill-label {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        font-family: 'AdobeClean-Bold', adobe-clean, sans-serif;
        font-weight: 400; /* Use Bold font file directly, not synthetic bold */
        font-size: 12px;
        line-height: 15px; /* 1.252 * 12px = 15.024px */
        color: #222222; /* Default text color from Figma Alias/content/neutral/default */
        padding: 4px 9px 5px 0; /* Reduced right padding to prevent wrapping */
        text-align: left;
        white-space: normal; /* Allow text wrapping for multi-line content */
        box-sizing: border-box;
        flex: 1;
        overflow: visible;
        -webkit-font-smoothing: subpixel-antialiased; /* Better font rendering */
        -moz-osx-font-smoothing: auto;
    }
